Current source of the opcode bodies:\n\n```cpp\n[[gnu::always_inline]]\ninline void op_filter_svf_lp(ExecutionContext& ctx, const Instruction& inst) {\n    float* out_l = ctx.buffers->get(inst.out_buffer);\n    float* out_r = ctx.buffers->get(static_cast<std::uint16_t>(inst.out_buffer + 1));\n    const float* input = ctx.buffers->get(inst.inputs[0]);\n    const bool stereo_in = (inst.flags & InstructionFlag::STEREO_INPUT) != 0;\n    const float* input_r = stereo_in\n        ? input[i] : (stereo_in ? input_r[i] : input[i]);\n            float v3 = x - (state.ic2eq[ch] + DENORMAL_DC);\n            float v1 = state.a1 * (state.ic1eq[ch] + DENORMAL_DC) + state.a2 * v3;\n            float v2 = (state.ic2eq[ch] + DENORMAL_DC) + state.a2 * (state.ic1eq[ch] + DENORMAL_DC) + state.a3 * v3;\n            state.ic1eq[ch] = clamp_audio(2.0f * v1 - state.ic1eq[ch]);\n            state.ic2eq[ch] = clamp_audio(2.0f * v2 - state.ic2eq[ch]);\n            (ch == 0 ? out_l : out_r)[i] = drywet::mix(x, v2, dry, wet);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n[[gnu::always_inline]]\ninline void op_filter_svf_hp(ExecutionContext& ctx, const Instruction& inst) {\n    float* out_l = ctx.buffers->get(inst.out_buffer);\n    float* out_r = ctx.buffers->get(static_cast<std::uint16_t>(inst.out_buffer + 1));\n    const float* input = ctx.buffers->get(inst.inputs[0]);\n    const bool stereo_in = (inst.flags & InstructionFlag::STEREO_INPUT) != 0;\n    const float* input_r = stereo_in\n        ? Keep\nthe scalar body and add a kernel in front of it:\n\n```cpp\n#include \"../dsp/simd.hpp\"\n\nnamespace kernels {\n#if CEDAR_SIMD_X86\nCEDAR_TARGET_AVX2 inline void mul_avx2(const float* a, const float* b, float* out) noexcept {\n    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < BLOCK_SIZE; i += 8)\n        _mm256_store_ps(out + i, _mm256_mul_ps(_mm256_load_ps(a + i), _mm256_load_ps(b + i)));\n}\n#endif\n}\n\ninline void op_mul(ExecutionContext& ctx, const Instruction& inst) {\n    // ... resolve a, b, out ...\n#if CEDAR_SIMD_X86\n    if (simd::has(simd::Isa::Avx2)) { kernels::mul_avx2(a, b, out); return; }\n#endif\n    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < BLOCK_SIZE; ++i) out[i] = a[i] * b[i];  // fallback stays\n}\n```\n\n- `CEDAR_TARGET_AVX2` is `[[gnu::target(\"avx2\")]]`; no compiler flags change.\n  FMA is **not** enabled (it would change rounding).\n- Buffers from `ctx.buffers->get()` are 32-byte aligned; `BLOCK_SIZE` is 128.\n- You may also add NEON (`CEDAR_SIMD_NEON`) or WASM SIMD128\n  (`CEDAR_SIMD_WASM`) paths; they are measured later on other machines, not\n  gated here.\n\n## Rules (enforced by the verifier \u2014 violations are rejected automatically)\n\n1. Features nonlinear saturation in the feedback path for analog character\n   175\t[[gnu::always_inline]]\n   176\tinline void op_filter_moog(ExecutionContext& ctx, const Instruction& inst) {\n   177\t input_scale_in[i] > 0.0f ? input_scale_in[i] : MOOG_INPUT_SCALE_DEFAULT;\n   208\t        float dry = drywet::coeff(dry_buf, i, dry_const);\n   209\t        float wet = drywet::coeff(wet_buf, i, wet_const);\n   210\t\n   211\t        // Update coefficients if parameters changed (shared across channels)\n   212\t        if (cutoff != state.last_freq || resonance != state.last_res) {\n   213\t            state.last_freq = cutoff;\n   214\t            state.last_res = resonance;\n   215\t\n   216\t            // Frequency warping for digital implementation\n   217\t            // Clamp cutoff to prevent instability at very high frequencies\n   218\t            float f = std::clamp(cutoff / ctx.sample_rate, 0.0f, 0.45f);\n   219\t\n   220\t            // Compute g coefficient using tan for frequency warping\n   221\t            state.g = std::tan(PI * f);\n   222\t\n   223\t            // Resonance coefficient (0 to max_resonance range, self-oscillates near 4)\n   224\t            state.k = std::clamp(resonance, 0.0f, max_resonance);\n   225\t        }\n   226\t\n   227\t        // G = g / (1 + g) for each stage (shared)\n   228\t        float G = state.g / (1.0f + state.g);\n from last stage output with nonlinear saturation\n   234\t            float feedback = state.k * soft_clip(state.stage[ch][3]);\n   235\t\n   236\t            // Input with feedback subtracted (negative feedback loop)\n   237\t            float x = xin - feedback;\n   238\t\n   239\t            // Soft clip the input to prevent harsh clipping at high input levels\n   240\t            x = soft_clip(x * input_scale) * (1.0f / input_scale);\n   241\t\n   242\t            // 4 cascaded 1-pole lowpass stages using trapezoidal integration\n   243\t            for (int j = 0; j < 4; ++j) {\n ============================================================================\n   270\t\n   271\t// Diode nonlinearity: hyperbolic sine approximation\n   272\t// True diode: I = I_s * (exp(V / V_t) - 1), but sinh provides symmetric behavior\n   273\t[[gnu::always_inline]]\n   274\tinline float diode_sinh(float x) {\n   275\t    // Fast sinh approximation for |x| < 4\n   276\t    // sinh(x) = x + x^3/6 + x^5/120 for small x\n   277\t    // For larger values, use (exp(x) - exp(-x)) / 2\n   278\t    if (x > 4.0f) return 27.29f + 0.5f * std::exp(x);  // exp(4)/2 \u2248 27.29\n   279\t    if (x < (used when inputs provide 0 or negative values)\n   296\t// VT: Thermal voltage - affects diode curve sharpness (real diode ~0.026V)\n   297\t// FB_GAIN: Feedback multiplier to compensate for VT attenuation in feedback path\n   298\tconstexpr float DIODE_VT_DEFAULT = 0.026f;\n   299\tconstexpr float DIODE_FB_GAIN_DEFAULT = 10.0f;\n   300\t\n   301\t// FILTER_DIODE: ZDF 4-pole diode ladder  // draws per-stimulus variations in hidden mode\n    59\t\n    60\tfloat frac(std::size_t n, std::size_t period) {\n    61\t    return static_cast<float>(n % period) / static_cast<float>(period);\n    62\t}\n    63\t\n    64\t// Audio-rate signal input: seeded noise \u2192 log sine sweep \u2192 impulse train \u2192\n    65\t// ramp, a quarter of the stimulus window each (PRD \u00a74.3 fixed stimuli).\n    66\tstruct AudioStim {\n    67\t    float gain;\n    68\t    std::vector<float> noise;\n    69\t    explicit AudioStim(float g) : gain(g), noise(STIM_BLOCKS * BLOCK_SIZE) {\n    70\t        std::mt19937 rng(g_seed ? g_seed : 0xCEDA5u);\n    71\t        std::uniform_real_distribution<float> d(-1.0f, 1.0f);\n    72\t        for (auto& x : noise) x = d(rng);\n    73\t        if (g_seed) make_hidden(rng);\n    74\t    }\n    75\t    // Hidden mode: 8 segments, each a random choice of noise at a random\n    76\t    // gain (from near-denormal to 4x the nominal level), sparse random\n    77\t    // impulses, DC steps, or silence. Slabs are allocated lazily so the pool can\n    31\t// grow on hot-swap without invalidating buffer pointers the audio thread\n    32\t// is reading: existing `std::unique_ptr<Slab>` entries never move, and\n    33\t// fresh slabs are filled in by the compile thread before the new program\n    34\t// is atomically published (so the audio thread only reads slab indices\n    35\t// the compile thread has already armed).\n    36\t//\n    37\t// Slab 0 is preallocated by the VM constructor so BUFFER_ZERO (slot 255)\n    38\t// is always valid.\n    39\tstruct BufferPool {\n    40\t    struct alignas(32) Slab {\n    41\t        std::array<std::array<float, BLOCK_SIZE>, SLAB_BUFFERS> data{};\n    42\t    };\n    43\t\n    44\t    BufferPool() {\n    45\t        // Preallocate slab 0 so indices < SLAB_BUFFERS (including\n    46\t        // BUFFER_ZERO = 255) are always live from VM construction\n    47\t        // onwards. The kernels keep the\n    52\t// recurrence serial (it must be \u2014 ic1eq/ic2eq feed back sample-to-sample)\n    53\t// but:\n    54\t//\n    55\t//   1. Precompute the whole block of SVF coefficients up front with 8-wide\n    56\t//      float vectors, exactly mirroring the operand grouping of `calc_svf`.\n    57\t//      IEEE float ops with the same tree shape and no FMA give bit-identical\n    58\t//      coefficients, except `tan` \u2014 see tan8_ps below.\n    59\t//   2. Run L and R of each sample in the two lanes of one __m128, with\n    60\t//      clamp_audio() expressed as NaN-mask + min/max (identical semantics).\n    61\t//\n    62\t// Denormals are never flushed: DAZ/FTZ are not touched, and every arithmetic\n    63\t// instruction used rounds exactly like the scalar SSE2 code.\n    64\t// ============================================================================\n    65\t\n    66\t#if CEDAR_SIMD_X86\n    67\tnamespace svf_kernels {\n    68\t\n    69\tinline constexpr std::size_t W = 8;  // AVX2 float lane count\n    70\t\n    71\t// tan(x) for x in [0.0013, 1.5394] (= PI*freq/sr, freq clamped to\n    72\t// [20, sr*0.49]).\n    73\t// simd: approx \u2014 replaces std::tan (tanf) in the SVF coefficient path.\n    74\t// Maclaurin sin and cos series in double precision (the argument is well\n    75\t// inside the convergence radius; terms through x^21 and x^22 keep the\n    76\t// truncation error below 1e-18 relative) followed by one division. Total\n    77\t// error < ~2e-15 relative, so the result rounds to the float nearest\n    78\t// tan(x) \u2014 the same value the scalar `std::tan` returns for these\n    79\t// arguments. No FMA is used (plain mul/sub), so the code is correct on any\n    80\t// AVX2 CPU.\n    81\tinline CEDAR_TARGET_AVX2 __m256d tan4_pd(__m256d v) noexcept {\n    82\t    const __m256d t = _mm256_mul_pd(v, v);\n    83\t    // sin(v) = v * (1 - t*(1/3! - t*(1/5! - ... - t*(1/25!))))\n    84\t    __m256d s = _mm256_set1_pd(1.0 / 15511210043330985984000000.0);   // 1/25!\n    85\t    s = _mm256_sub_pd(_mm256_set1_pd(1.0 / 25852016738884976640000.0), _mm256_mul_pd(t, s));  // 1/23!\n    86\t    s = _mm256_sub_pd(_mm256_set1_pd(1.0 / 51090942171709440000.0), _mm256_mul_pd(t, s));     // 1/21!\n    87\t    s = _mm256_sub_pd(_mm256_set1_pd(1.0 / 121645100408832000.0), _mm256_mul_pd(t, s));       // 1/19!\n    88\t    s = _mm256_sub_pd(_mm256_set1_pd(1.0 / 355687428096000.0), _mm256_mul_pd(t, s));         // 1/17!\n    89\t    s = _mm256_sub_pd(_mm256_set1_pd(1.0 / 1307674368000.0), _mm256_mul_pd(t, s));           // 1/15!\n    90\t    s = _mm256_sub_pd(_mm256_set1_pd(1.0 / 6227020800.0), _mm256_mul_pd(t, s));              // 1/13!\n    91\t    s = _mm256_sub_pd(_mm256_set1_pd(1.0 / 39916800.0), _mm256_mul_pd(t, s));                // 1/11!\n    92\t    s = _mm256_sub_pd(_mm256_set1_pd(1.0 / 362880.0), _mm256_mul_pd(t, s));                  // 1/9!\n    93\t    s = _mm256_sub_pd(_mm256_set1_pd(1.0 / 5040.0), _mm256_mul_pd(t, s));                    // 1/7!\n    94\t    s = _mm256_sub_pd(_mm256_set1_pd(1.0 / 120.0), _mm256_mul_pd(t, s));                     // 1/5!\n    95\t    s = _mm256_sub_pd(_mm256_set1_pd(1.0 / 6.0), _mm256_mul_pd(t, s));                       // 1/3!\n    96\t    s = _mm256_sub_pd(_mm256_set1_pd(1.0), _mm256_mul_pd(t, s));                             // S\n    97\t    s = _mm256_mul_pd(v, s);                                                                 // sin\n    98\t    // cos(v) = 1 - t*(1/2! - t*(1/4! - ... - t*(1/26!)))\n    99\t    __m256d c = _mm256_set1_pd(1.0 / 403291461126605635584000000.0);  // 1/26!\n   100\t    c = _mm256_sub_pd(_mm256_set1_pd(1.0 / 620448401733239439360000.0), _mm256_mul_pd(t, c)); // 1/24!\n   101\t    c = _mm256_sub_pd(_mm256_set1_pd(1.0 / 1124000727777607680000.0), _mm256_mul_pd(t, c));   // 1/22!\n   102\t    c = _mm256_sub_pd(_mm256_set1_pd(1.0 / 2432902008176640000.0), _mm256_mul_pd(t, c));      // 1/20!\n   103\t    c = _mm256_sub_pd(_mm256_set1_pd(1.0 / 6402373705728000.0), _mm256_mul_pd(t, c));        // 1/18!\n   104\t    c = _mm256_sub_pd(_mm256_set1_pd(1.0 / 20922789888000.0), _mm256_mul_pd(t, c));          // 1/16!\n   105\t    c = _mm256_sub_pd(_mm256_set1_pd(1.0 / 87178291200.0), _mm256_mul_pd(t, c));             // 1/14!\n   106\t    c = _mm256_sub_pd(_mm256_set1_pd(1.0 / 479001600.0), _mm256_mul_pd(t, c));               // 1/12!\n   107\t    c = _mm256_sub_pd(_mm256_set1_pd(1.0 / 3628800.0), _mm256_mul_pd(t, c));                 // 1/10!\n   108\t    c = _mm256_sub_pd(_mm256_set1_pd(1.0 / 40320.0), _mm256_mul_pd(t, c));                   // 1/8!\n   109\t    c = _mm256_sub_pd(_mm256_set1_pd(1.0 / 720.0), _mm256_mul_pd(t, c));                     // 1/6!\n   110\t    c = _mm256_sub_pd(_mm256_set1_pd(1.0 / 24.0), _mm256_mul_pd(t, c));                      // 1/4!\n   111\t    c = _mm256_sub_pd(_mm256_set1_pd(1.0 / 2.0), _mm256_mul_pd(t, c));                       // 1/2!\n   112\t    c = _mm256_sub_pd(_mm256_set1_pd(1.0), _mm256_mul_pd(t, c));                             // cos\n   113\t    return _mm256_div_pd(s, c);                                                              // tan\n   114\t}\n   115\t\n   116\t// tan for 8 float lanes at once (see tan4_pd for the error bound).\n   117\tinline CEDAR_TARGET_AVX2 __m256 tan8_ps(__m256 x) noexcept {\n   118\t    const __m256d r0 = tan4_pd(_mm256_cvtps_pd(_mm256_castps256_ps128(x)));\n   119\t    const __m256d r1 = tan4_pd(_mm256_cvtps_pd(_mm256_extractf128_ps(x, 1)));\n   120\t    return _mm256_set_m128(_mm256_cvtpd_ps(r1), _mm256_cvtpd_ps(r0));\n   121\t}\n   122\t\n   123\t// Per-block SVF coefficients: g, k, a1, a2, a3 for every sample, computed with\n   124\t// 8-wide vectors. Mirrors calc_svf() exactly, except that the tan/freq-change\n   125\t// cache is irrelevant (recomputing identical coefficients gives identical\n   126\t// values) and tan comes from tan8_ps.\n   127\tCEDAR_TARGET_AVX2 inline void coefs_avx2(const float* freq, const float* q,\n   128\t                                         float sample_rate,\n   129\t                                         float* g, float* k, float* a1,\n   130\t                                         float* a2, float* a3) noexcept {\n   131\t    CEDAR_SIMD_ASSERT_ALIGNED(freq, 32);\n   132\t    CEDAR_SIMD_ASSERT_ALIGNED(q, 32);\n   133\t    const __m256 zero = _mm256_setzero_ps();\n   134\t    const __m256 fmin = _mm256_set1_ps(20.0f);\n   135\t    const __m256 fmax = _mm256_set1_ps(sample_rate * 0.49f);\n   136\t    const __m256 qmin = _mm256_set1_ps(0.1f);\n   137\t    const __m256 piv = _mm256_set1_ps(PI);\n   138\t    const __m256 srv = _mm256_set1_ps(sample_rate);\n   139\t    const __m256 one = _mm256_set1_ps(1.0f);\n   140\t    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < BLOCK_SIZE; i += W) {\n   141\t        // freq = max(0, freq); clamp(20, sr*0.49); q = max(0.1, q).\n   142\t        // maxps returns the second operand when the first is NaN \u2014 the same\n   143\t        // value std::max(0.0f, x) / std::max(0.1f, x) produce for NaN input.\n   144\t        __m256 f = _mm256_max_ps(_mm256_load_ps(freq + i), zero);\n   145\t        f = _mm256_max_ps(f, fmin);\n   146\t        f = _mm256_min_ps(f, fmax);\n   147\t        const __m256 qv = _mm256_max_ps(_mm256_load_ps(q + i), qmin);\n   148\t        // tan(PI * freq / sample_rate) \u2014 same operand grouping as the scalar.\n   149\t        const __m256 gv = tan8_ps(_mm256_div_ps(_mm256_mul_ps(piv, f), srv));\n   150\t        const __m256 kv = _mm256_div_ps(one, qv);\n   151\t        const __m256 a1v = _mm256_div_ps(one, _mm256_add_ps(one, _mm256_mul_ps(gv, _mm256_add_ps(gv, kv))));\n   152\t        const __m256 a2v = _mm256_mul_ps(gv, a1v);\n   153\t        const __m256 a3v = _mm256_mul_ps(gv, a2v);\n   154\t        _mm256_store_ps(g + i, gv);\n   155\t        _mm256_store_ps(k + i, kv);\n   156\t        _mm256_store_ps(a1 + i, a1v);\n   157\t        _mm256_store_ps(a2 + i, a2v);\n   158\t        _mm256_store_ps(a3 + i, a3v);\n   159\t    }\n   160\t}\n   161\t\n   162\t// mode: 0 = lowpass (v2), 1 = highpass (x - k*v1 - v2), 2 = bandpass (v1).\n   163\t// The two channels run in the two lanes of one __m128; every expression uses\n   164\t// the scalar body's exact grouping, so the recurrence is bit-identical\n   165\t// (including denormals \u2014 nothing here flushes them).\n   166\t[[gnu::always_inline]]\n   167\tCEDAR_TARGET_AVX2 inline void run_avx2(SVFState& state,\n   168\t                                       const float* in_l, const float* in_r,\n   169\t                                       const float* freq, const float* q,\n   170\t                                       const float* dry_buf, const float* wet_buf,\n   171\t                                       float* out_l, float* out_r,\n   172\t                                       float sample_rate, int mode) noexcept {\n   173\t    CEDAR_SIMD_ASSERT_ALIGNED(out_l, 32);\n   174\t    CEDAR_SIMD_ASSERT_ALIGNED(out_r, 32);\n   175\t    alignas(32) float g[BLOCK_SIZE], kk[BLOCK_SIZE];\n   176\t    alignas(32) float a1[BLOCK_SIZE], a2[BLOCK_SIZE], a3[BLOCK_SIZE];\n   177\t    coefs_avx2(freq, q, sample_rate, g, kk, a1, a2, a3);\n   178\t\n   179\t    // Read the cache keys before any output write, in case an output buffer\n   180\t    // aliases freq/q (the scalar body reads them at the top of each sample).\n   181\t    const float last_f = std::max(0.0f, freq[BLOCK_SIZE - 1]);\n   182\t    const float last_q = q[BLOCK_SIZE - 1];\n   183\t\n   184\t    __m128 ic1 = _mm_setr_ps(state.ic1eq[0], state.ic1eq[1], 0.0f, 0.0f);\n   185\t    __m128 ic2 = _mm_setr_ps(state.ic2eq[0], state.ic2eq[1], 0.0f, 0.0f);\n   186\t    const __m128 dc = _mm_set1_ps(DENORMAL_DC);\n   187\t    const __m128 two = _mm256_castps256_ps128(_mm256_set1_ps(2.0f));\n   188\t    const __m128 ten = _mm256_castps256_ps128(_mm256_set1_ps(10.0f));\n   189\t    const __m128 nten = _mm256_castps256_ps128(_mm256_set1_ps(-10.0f));\n   190\t    alignas(16) float tmp[4];\n   191\t\n   192\t    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < BLOCK_SIZE; ++i) {\n   193\t        const float xl = in_l[i];\n   194\t        const float xr = in_r ? in_r[i] : xl;\n   195\t        const __m128 x = _mm_setr_ps(xl, xr, 0.0f, 0.0f);\n   196\t        const __m128 ic1p = _mm_add_ps(ic1, dc);\n   197\t        const __m128 ic2p = _mm_add_ps(ic2, dc);\n   198\t        const __m128 v3 = _mm_sub_ps(x, ic2p);\n   199\t        const __m128 A1 = _mm_set1_ps(a1[i]);\n   200\t        const __m128 A2 = _mm_set1_ps(a2[i]);\n   201\t        const __m128 A3 = _mm_set1_ps(a3[i]);\n   202\t        const __m128 v1 = _mm_add_ps(_mm_mul_ps(A1, ic1p), _mm_mul_ps(A2, v3));\n   203\t        const __m128 v2 = _mm_add_ps(_mm_add_ps(ic2p, _mm_mul_ps(A2, ic1p)),\n   204\t                                     _mm_mul_ps(A3, v3));\n   205\t        // clamp_audio(2*v1 - ic1) / clamp_audio(2*v2 - ic2):\n   206\t        // NaN -> 0 via the unordered mask (AND-NOT), then min/max clamp \u2014\n   207\t        // identical results to the scalar branches for every input.\n   208\t        __m128 n1 = _mm_sub_ps(_mm_mul_ps(two, v1), ic1);\n   209\t        __m128 n2 = _mm_sub_ps(_mm_mul_ps(two, v2), ic2);\n   210\t        n1 = _mm_min_ps(_mm_max_ps(_mm_andnot_ps(_mm_cmpunord_ps(n1, n1), n1), nten), ten);\n   211\t        n2 = _mm_min_ps(_mm_max_ps(_mm_andnot_ps(_mm_cmpunord_ps(n2, n2), n2), nten), ten);\n   212\t        ic1 = n1;\n   213\t        ic2 = n2;\n   214\t        __m128 sig;\n   215\t        if (mode == 0) {\n   216\t            sig = v2;  // lowpass\n   217\t        } else if (mode == 1) {\n   218\t            sig = _mm_sub_ps(_mm_sub_ps(x, _mm_mul_ps(_mm_set1_ps(kk[i]), v1)), v2);  // highpass\n   219\t        } else {\n   220\t            sig = v1;  // bandpass\n   221\t        }\n   222\t        const float dry = dry_buf ? dry_buf[i] : 0.0f;\n   223\t        const float wet = wet_buf ? wet_buf[i] : 1.0f;\n   224\t        const __m128 o = _mm_add_ps(_mm_mul_ps(x, _mm_set1_ps(dry)),\n   225\t                                    _mm_mul_ps(sig, _mm_set1_ps(wet)));\n   226\t        _mm_store_ps(tmp, o);\n   227\t        out_l[i] = tmp[0];\n   228\t        out_r[i] = tmp[1];\n   229\t    }\n   230\t    _mm_store_ps(tmp, ic1);\n   231\t    state.ic1eq[0] = tmp[0];\n   232\t    state.ic1eq[1] = tmp[1];\n   233\t    _mm_store_ps(tmp, ic2);\n   234\t    state.ic2eq[0] = tmp[0];\n   235\t    state.ic2eq[1] = tmp[1];\n   236\t    // Keep the shared coefficient cache consistent with the scalar body.\n   237\t    state.g = g[BLOCK_SIZE - 1];\n   238\t    state.k = kk[BLOCK_SIZE - 1];\n   239\t    state.a1 = a1[BLOCK_SIZE - 1];\n   240\t    state.a2 = a2[BLOCK_SIZE - 1];"
